====================================================================================================
                               AQUACARE SYSTEM - TEST CASES SPECIFICATION
                  HE THONG QUAN LY VA GIAM SAT HO CA THUY SINH THONG MINH IOT
====================================================================================================

TAI LIEU: BANG KHOA MUC & CHI TIET CAC TEST CASES KIEM THU CHAT LUONG HE THONG AQUACARE
PHIEN BAN: 2.5.0-RELEASE
NGAY KHOI TAO: 17/10/2026
NGUOI THUC HIEN: Phan Tri Dung (AquaCare Engineering Team)
MUC DICH: Cung cap day du danh muc cac kich ban kiem thu (Test Cases) chi tiet tu cap do
          Unit Test, Integration Test, Functional Test, E2E Test, Security Test, Performance
          den UI/UX tren ca 3 nen tang: AquaCare Web, AquaCare Mobile App va IoT Firmware Backend.
QUY DINH KIEM THU:
  - Moi Test Case phai duoc thuc hien theo dung cac buoc tien hanh (Test Steps).
  - Ket qua mong doi (Expected Results) phai duoc doi chieu voi du lieu thuc te trong CSDL.
  - Moi loi phat sinh trong qua trinh kiem thu phai duoc tao Bug Ticket tuong ung kem Test Case ID.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DANH MUC CAC PHAN HE & MODULES TRONG TAI LIEU KIEM THU:
  01. Module [AUTH]: Xac Thuc & Phan Quyen (Authentication & Authorization)
  02. Module [PROFILE]: Quan Ly Ho So & Thong Tin Ca Nhan (Profile Management)
  03. Module [SPECIES]: Quan Ly Danh Muc Loai Ca & Nguong Thong So (Fish Species Management)
  04. Module [TANK]: Quan Ly Ho Thuy Sinh & Be Ca (Aquarium Tank Management)
  05. Module [DEVICE]: Quan Ly Thiet Bi IoT & Ket Noi Phan Cung (IoT Device & Hardware Pairing)
  06. Module [RELAY]: Dieu Khien Relay & Hen Gio Thiet Bi Ngoai Vi (Relay Control & Schedules)
  07. Module [CALIB]: Quy Trinh Hieu Chuan Cam Bien (Sensor Calibration Workflow)
  08. Module [TELEMETRY]: Thu Thap Du Lieu Cam Bien & Giam Sat Truc Quan (Telemetry Ingestion & Monitoring)
  09. Module [ALERT]: He Thong Phat Hien Vi Pham & Canh Bao Nguong (Alert Engine & Violations)
  10. Module [NOTI]: Trung Tam Thong Bao & Push Notification (Notifications & FCM Engine)
  11. Module [ECOMMERCE]: Gian Hang Thiet Bi & Phu Kien Thuy Sinh (Products & Catalog)
  12. Module [CART]: Gio Hang & Dat Hang Thanh Toan (Cart & Checkout Workflow)
  13. Module [ORDER]: Xu Ly Don Hang & Dieu Phoi Giao Nhan (Order Processing & Logistics)
  14. Module [STAFF]: Nghiep Vu Nhan Vien & Phan He Chuyen Biet (Staff Operations & Portals)
  15. Module [MOBILE]: Ung Dung AquaCare Mobile App (Mobile App UI/UX & Native Features)
  16. Module [SECURITY]: Bao Mat, Hieu Nang & Kiem Thu Bien (Security, Performance & Boundary)
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


####################################################################################################
PHAN HE 01: XAC THUC & PHAN QUYEN (AUTHENTICATION & AUTHORIZATION)
MA PHAN HE: AUTH
SO LUONG TEST CASE CO SO: 15
####################################################################################################

==========================================================================================
TEST CASE ID      : TC_AUTH_001
TIEU DE TEST CASE : Dang ky tai khoan moi voi email va mat khau hop le
PHAN HE / MODULE  : AUTH - Xac Thuc & Phan Quyen (Authentication & Authorization)
MUC DO UU TIEN    : Critical
LOAI KIEM THU     : Functional
TRANG THAI        : Ready for Testing
NGUOI PHU TRACH   : QA / Tester Team
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. DIEU KIEN TIEN QUYET (PRECONDITIONS):
   Chua co tai khoan voi email test@aquacare.vn trong he thong
2. DU LIEU KIEM THU (TEST DATA / INPUT):
   fullName: Nguyen Van A, email: test@aquacare.vn, phone: 0901234567, password: AquaCare@2026
3. CAC BUOC THUC HIEN (TEST STEPS):
   B1: Truy cap man hinh Dang ky
   B2: Nhap Ho va ten: Nguyen Van A
   B3: Nhap Email: test@aquacare.vn
   B4: Nhap So dien thoai: 0901234567
   B5: Nhap Mat khau: AquaCare@2026
   B6: Nhan nut Dang ky
4. KET QUA MONG DOI (EXPECTED RESULT):
   Tai khoan duoc tao thanh cong tren Supabase Auth va bang public.users, he thong gui email xac thuc hoac dang nhap thanh cong.
5. TIEU CHI DANH GIA PASSED / FAILED:
   [x] He thong phan hoi dung ma HTTP status code (200, 201, 400, 401, 403, v.v.).
   [x] Du lieu trong database (PostgreSQL / Supabase) khop hoan toan voi thong tin nhap.
   [x] Khong xuat hien unhandled exception, crash app hoac man hinh trang (white screen).
6. KET QUA THUC TE (ACTUAL RESULT):
   Chua kiem thu / San sang test tren moi truong Staging & Production.
7. GHI CHU THEM (NOTES):
   Can luu y kiem tra dong bo giua Web, Mobile App va IoT Simulator khi thuc thi.
==========================================================================================

TEST CASE BIEN THE: TC_AUTH_002 - [Dang ky tai khoan moi voi email va mat khau hop le] - Kiem thu do tre va thoi gian phan hoi (Latency SLA)
LOAI KIEM THU     : Non-Functional / UI-UX / Performance
MUC DO UU TIEN    : Medium
BUOC THUC HIEN    : Thuc hien lai quy trinh TC_AUTH_001 duoi dieu kien dac thu (Kiem thu do tre va thoi gian phan hoi (Latency SLA)).
KET QUA MONG DOI  : Latency < 500ms tren mang 4G/WiFi binh thuong.
DANH GIA          : To be verified by QA team.
